61:    public ReplayStats replay(long runId, String symbol, Instant executionStart, Instant executionEnd,
62:                              List<TradeSignal> generatedSignals,
63:                              List<MarketPriceEventService.PriceEvent> productionPriceEvents,
64:                              BooleanSupplier stopRequested) {
65:        List<TradeSignal> timeline = generatedSignals.stream()
66:                .filter(s -> s != null && s.getGeneratedAt() != null)
67:                .sorted(Comparator.comparing(TradeSignal::getGeneratedAt)
68:                        .thenComparingInt(s -> intervalOrder(s.getInterval())))
69:                .toList();
70:
71:        TradeSignal latest1m = null;
72:        TradeSignal latest5m = null;
73:        TradeSignal latest1h = null;
74:        ShadowPosition open = null;
75:        BigDecimal cash = INITIAL_CAPITAL;
76:        BigDecimal realized = BigDecimal.ZERO;
77:        int trades = 0, wins = 0, losses = 0;
78:        int consecutiveFinalOneMinuteStrongSells = 0;
79:        WalletSettings replayWalletSettings = walletSettings();
80:        BigDecimal replayDailyBudget = executionSizingPolicy.initialDailyBudget(
81:                cash, replayWalletSettings.getMinimumUsdtReserve(),
82:                replayWalletSettings.getBaseTradeAmountUsdt(), replayWalletSettings.getMaximumDailyNewPositions());
83:        LocalDate replayTradeDate = null;
84:        int replayExecutedNewPositions = 0;
85:        // FIX-052: when Production live-price events exist, Replay uses them as the
86:        // authoritative mechanical-protection clock. Older windows created before V64
87:        // transparently retain candle/signal-close fallback and are therefore not claimed
88:        // as tick-exact parity runs.
89:        List<MarketPriceEventService.PriceEvent> livePrices = productionPriceEvents == null
90:                ? List.of()
91:                : productionPriceEvents.stream()
92:                    .filter(e -> e != null && e.observedAt() != null && e.price() != null)
93:                    .sorted(Comparator.comparing(MarketPriceEventService.PriceEvent::observedAt))
94:                    .toList();
95:        boolean exactPriceReplay = !livePrices.isEmpty();
96:        int livePriceIndex = 0;
97:        // FIX-109: normal Regression/Replay runs are Production-Parity by default.
98:        // Experimental rules require an explicitly opened EXPERIMENTAL scope.
99:        String replayLogicMode = ExecutionReplayScope.ReplayLogicMode.PRODUCTION_PARITY.name();
100:        defensiveRiskReductionObserver.logRunStart(runId, symbol);
101:        oneCandleContinuationGraceReplayObserver.startRun(runId, symbol);
102:
103:        try (ExecutionReplayScope.Scope ignored = replayScope.open(runId, timeline,
104:                opportunity -> {
105:                    TradeSignal latest = opportunity == null ? null : opportunity.getLatestSignal();
106:                    if (latest != null && latest.getGeneratedAt() != null
107:                            && !latest.getGeneratedAt().isBefore(executionStart)
108:                            && !latest.getGeneratedAt().isAfter(executionEnd)) {
109:                        persistProductionOpportunity(runId, opportunity);
110:                    }
111:                })) {
112:        // FIX-122: independent OLD comparison revision; ordinary Replay defaults to NEW.
113:        java.util.List<Boolean> revisions = jdbcTemplate.query(
114:                "SELECT enabled FROM fix122_replay_revision WHERE test_run_id=?",
115:                (rs,row) -> rs.getBoolean(1), runId);
116:        replayScope.fix122Enabled(revisions == null || revisions.isEmpty() || revisions.get(0));
117:        for (TradeSignal signal : timeline) {
118:            // FIX-090: shadow execution is frequently cancellable without affecting Production.
119:            if (stopRequested != null && stopRequested.getAsBoolean()) {
120:                throw new ReplayCancellationException(runId);
121:            }
122:            // FIX-052 exact ordering: BinanceKlineService invokes live position protection
123:            // from the 1m price update BEFORE publishing the candle-close analysis event.
124:            // Consume all persisted Production prices up to this signal timestamp before
125:            // making the freshly generated signal visible to position management.
126:            while (livePriceIndex < livePrices.size()
127:                    && (stopRequested == null || !stopRequested.getAsBoolean())
128:                    && !livePrices.get(livePriceIndex).observedAt().isAfter(signal.getGeneratedAt())) {
129:                MarketPriceEventService.PriceEvent live = livePrices.get(livePriceIndex++);
130:                replayScope.referencePrice(symbol, live.observedAt(), live.price());
131:                // FIX-11Q: advance only the isolated counterfactual grace position. This never mutates
132:                // the baseline Shadow position/cash/trades and therefore cannot change Replay parity.
133:                oneCandleContinuationGraceReplayObserver.onPrice(runId, symbol, live, latest1m, latest5m, latest1h);
134:                if (open != null && !live.observedAt().isBefore(executionStart) && !live.observedAt().isAfter(executionEnd)) {
135:                    LivePriceEvaluation protection = evaluateLivePrice(runId, symbol, open, live, latest1m, latest5m, latest1h);
136:                    open = protection.position();
137:                    if (protection.partialProceeds().signum() > 0) {
138:                        cash = cash.add(protection.partialProceeds(), MC);
139:                        realized = realized.add(protection.partialRealizedPnl(), MC);
140:                    }
141:                    ExitDecision liveExit = protection.decision();
142:                    if (liveExit.exit()) {
143:                        BigDecimal proceeds = live.price().multiply(open.quantity(), MC);
144:                        BigDecimal pnl = proceeds.subtract(open.cost(), MC);
145:                        BigDecimal pnlPct = percentage(open.entryPrice(), live.price());
146:                        BigDecimal totalPositionPnl = open.partialRealizedPnl().add(pnl, MC);
147:                        BigDecimal totalPositionPnlPct = totalPositionPnlPercent(open, pnl);
148:                        cash = cash.add(proceeds, MC);
149:                        realized = realized.add(pnl, MC);
150:                        trades++;
151:                        if (totalPositionPnl.signum() > 0) wins++; else if (totalPositionPnl.signum() < 0) losses++;
152:                        // wallet_execution_test stores this terminal leg; wallet_position_test stores
153:                        // the whole lifecycle including any earlier FIX-11T partial realization.
154:                        persistSellAtPrice(runId, symbol, live, latest1m, open, liveExit, pnl, pnlPct);
155:                        closePositionAtPrice(runId, open.positionId(), live, liveExit, totalPositionPnl, totalPositionPnlPct);
156:                        replayScope.recordStop(symbol, open.positionId(), live.observedAt(), liveExit.reason());
157:                        executionIntelligenceService.completePositionOpportunity(symbol, latest1m, liveExit.reason());
158:                                                open = null;
159:                    }
160:                }
161:            }
162:
163:            replayScope.reference(signal.getGeneratedAt());
164:            if ("1m".equals(signal.getInterval())) {
165:                latest1m = signal;
166:                consecutiveFinalOneMinuteStrongSells = signal.getDecision() == SignalDecision.STRONG_SELL
167:                        ? consecutiveFinalOneMinuteStrongSells + 1 : 0;
168:            }
169:            if ("5m".equals(signal.getInterval())) latest5m = signal;
170:            if ("1h".equals(signal.getInterval())) latest1h = signal;
171:            oneCandleContinuationGraceReplayObserver.onSignal(runId, signal, latest1m, latest5m, latest1h);
172:
173:            // FIX-11K Phase A: observe only. This runs beside the existing exit path and cannot
174:            // mutate quantity, cash, wallet_position_test, SELL authority, or Production state.
175:            // It records enough replay-native evidence to evaluate the 2/3/4 streak and
176:            // peak/giveback experiment matrix after the run instead of pre-selecting thresholds.
177:            if (open != null && "1m".equals(signal.getInterval())) {
178:                defensiveRiskReductionObserver.observe(
179:                        runId, open.positionId(), signal, latest5m, latest1h,
180:                        consecutiveFinalOneMinuteStrongSells, open.entryPrice(), open.highest());
181:            }
182:
183:            if (open != null && !exactPriceReplay) {
184:                // Pre-V64 fallback only. Exact-parity runs receive mechanical protection from
185:                // the persisted Production live-price stream above.
186:                ExitDecision exit = evaluateExit(runId, open, signal, latest1m, latest5m, latest1h);
187:                if (exit.exit()) {
188:                    BigDecimal proceeds = signal.getLatestPrice().multiply(open.quantity(), MC);
189:                    BigDecimal pnl = proceeds.subtract(open.cost(), MC);
190:                    BigDecimal pnlPct = percentage(open.entryPrice(), signal.getLatestPrice());
191:                    BigDecimal totalPositionPnl = open.partialRealizedPnl().add(pnl, MC);
192:                    BigDecimal totalPositionPnlPct = totalPositionPnlPercent(open, pnl);
193:                    cash = cash.add(proceeds, MC);
194:                    realized = realized.add(pnl, MC);
195:                    trades++;
196:                    if (totalPositionPnl.signum() > 0) wins++; else if (totalPositionPnl.signum() < 0) losses++;
197:                    persistSell(runId, symbol, signal, open, exit, pnl, pnlPct);
198:                    closePosition(runId, open.positionId(), signal, exit, totalPositionPnl, totalPositionPnlPct);
199:                    // FIX-020 replay parity: a terminal replay exit consumes the same
200:                    // opportunity evidence boundary as production before any new BUY can form.
201:                    replayScope.recordStop(symbol, open.positionId(), signal.getGeneratedAt(), exit.reason());
202:                    executionIntelligenceService.completePositionOpportunity(symbol, signal, exit.reason());
203:                                        open = null;
204:                    // Production PaperTradingService returns immediately after a signal-driven
205:                    // terminal close; it cannot reopen from that same signal invocation.
206:                    continue;
207:                } else {
208:                    if (exit.newTakeProfit() != null) {
209:                        open = rebaseForExtendedTarget(open, exit.newTakeProfit(), signal.getLatestPrice(), signal.getGeneratedAt())
210:                                .withTakeProfit(exit.newTakeProfit());
211:                        persistProfitLockState(open);
212:                        persistNearTpState(open);
213:                    }
214:                    open = updateProfitLock(open, signal);
215:                    // FIX-11T: legacy signal-price fallback cannot be tick-exact, but it still
216:                    // executes the SAME Near-TP policy from the authoritative historical signal
217:                    // price so older Replay windows do not silently omit the Production rule.
218:                    if (priceAuthorityPolicy.canUseSignalPrice(signal, open.entryTime())) {
219:                        FallbackNearTpEvaluation nearTpFallback = evaluateNearTpAtSignalFallback(
220:                                runId, open, signal, latest1m, latest5m);
350:                int addPercent = Math.max(1, Math.min(100 - open.positionPercent(), decision.positionPercent()));
351:                WalletExecutionSizingPolicy.Plan sizing = executionSizingPolicy.plan(
352:                        cash, replayWalletSettings.getMinimumUsdtReserve(), replayDailyBudget,
353:                        addPercent, open.positionPercent(), false, replayWalletSettings.getMaximumDailyNewPositions(),
354:                        replayExecutedNewPositions, fresh.price());
355:                BigDecimal budget = sizing.spend();
356:                if (sizing.allowed()) {
357:                    addPercent = sizing.normalizedPositionPercent();
358:                    BigDecimal addedQty = sizing.quantity();
359:                    persistBuy(runId, symbol, signal, fresh, addedQty, budget, addPercent, decision);
360:                    executionIntelligenceService.markExecuted(signal, decision);
361:                    // FIX-112A: a progressive add consumes only its own triggering signal.
362:                    replayScope.markEntryConsumed(signal.getId());
363:                    cash = cash.subtract(budget, MC);
364:                    open = addToPosition(runId, open, signal, addedQty, budget, addPercent);
365:                }
366:            }
367:        }
368:        }
369:
370:        BigDecimal finalWallet = cash;
371:        if (open != null && !timeline.isEmpty()) {
372:            BigDecimal mark = timeline.get(timeline.size() - 1).getLatestPrice();
373:            if (mark != null) finalWallet = finalWallet.add(mark.multiply(open.quantity(), MC), MC);
374:        }
375:
376:        jdbcTemplate.update("""
377:                UPDATE analysis_test_run
378:                SET simulated_trade_count=?, simulated_win_count=?, simulated_loss_count=?,
379:                    simulated_realized_pnl=?, simulated_final_wallet=?
380:                WHERE id=?
381:                """, trades, wins, losses, realized, finalWallet, runId);
382:        defensiveRiskReductionObserver.logRunSummary(runId, symbol);
383:        oneCandleContinuationGraceReplayObserver.finishRun(runId, symbol, executionEnd);
384:        // FIX-109: expose whether this run had the persisted live-price stream required
385:        // for tick-exact production ordering. Older windows remain explicitly labelled
386:        // as fallback instead of being mistaken for exact parity.
387:        return new ReplayStats(trades, wins, losses, realized, finalWallet,
388:                exactPriceReplay ? "EXACT_PRICE_REPLAY" : "SIGNAL_PRICE_FALLBACK",
389:                replayLogicMode);
390:    }
391:
392:    /**
393:     * FIX-052: mechanical position protection evaluated from the exact Production
394:     * 1m live-price observation stream. The order mirrors LivePositionProtectionService:
395:     * TAKE_PROFIT -> STOP_LOSS -> PROFIT_LOCK -> normal HTF exit. The newly closed
396:     * 1m signal is intentionally not visible yet; BinanceKlineService calls onPrice()
397:     * before publishing the candle-close event in Production.
398:     */
399:    private LivePriceEvaluation evaluateLivePrice(long runId, String symbol, ShadowPosition p,
400:                                                   MarketPriceEventService.PriceEvent event,
401:                                                   TradeSignal oneMinute, TradeSignal five, TradeSignal one) {
402:        BigDecimal price = event.price();
403:        ShadowPosition current = p;
404:
405:        if (current.takeProfit() != null && price.compareTo(current.takeProfit()) >= 0) {
406:            PositionContinuationPolicy.Evaluation continuation = continuationPolicy.evaluate(
407:                    oneMinute, five, one,
408:                    current.entryTrend(), current.entryStructure(), current.entryMomentum(), current.entryVolume(),
409:                    current.entryConfidence(), current.entryScore());
410:            if (continuation.extendTarget()) {
500:            PositionExitPolicy.Evaluation lockDecision = exitPolicy.evaluateProfitLockBreach(oneMinute, five, one);
501:            if (lockDecision.exit()) {
502:                String reason = "Price retraced to the protected profit level after a profitable advance. "
503:                        + lockDecision.explanation() + " " + profitLockConfigText();
504:                persistManagementAtPrice(runId, symbol, event, "PROFIT_LOCK_EXIT", current.takeProfit(), current.takeProfit(), current, reason);
505:                return new LivePriceEvaluation(new ExitDecision(true, lockDecision.code(), reason), current);
506:            }
507:            persistManagementAtPrice(runId, symbol, event, "PROFIT_LOCK_HOLD", current.takeProfit(), current.takeProfit(), current,
508:                    lockDecision.explanation() + " " + profitLockConfigText());
509:        }
510:
511:        PositionExitPolicy.Evaluation normalExit = exitPolicy.evaluateNormalExit(oneMinute, five, one);
512:        if (normalExit.exit()) {
513:            return new LivePriceEvaluation(new ExitDecision(true, normalExit.code(), normalExit.explanation()), current);
514:        }
515:
516:        // FIX-11T: shared Production/Replay rule, evaluated LAST after all existing exits.
517:        NearTpFailureProtectionPolicy.State nearTpState = new NearTpFailureProtectionPolicy.State(
518:                current.nearTpState(), current.nearTpBestPrice(), current.nearTpBearishStreak(),
519:                current.nearTpLastOneMinuteSignalId(), current.nearTpHarvestUsed());
520:        NearTpFailureProtectionPolicy.Evaluation nearTp = nearTpFailureProtectionPolicy.evaluate(
521:                nearTpState, current.entryPrice(), current.takeProfit(), price, event.observedAt(), oneMinute, five);
522:        ShadowPosition nearTpUpdated = current.withNearTp(nearTp.state());
523:        if (!nearTpUpdated.sameNearTp(current)) {
524:            persistNearTpState(nearTpUpdated);
525:        }
526:        current = nearTpUpdated;
527:        if (nearTp.transition()) {
528:            log.info("FIX-11T Replay Near-TP: runId={}, positionId={}, symbol={}, code={}, state={}, price={}, best={}, givebackPct={}, bearish1mStreak={}, detail={}",
529:                    runId, current.positionId(), symbol, nearTp.code(), current.nearTpState(), price,
530:                    current.nearTpBestPrice(), nearTp.givebackPercent(), current.nearTpBearishStreak(), nearTp.explanation());
531:        }
532:        if (nearTp.harvestEligible()) {
533:            BigDecimal soldQty = current.quantity().multiply(BigDecimal.valueOf(0.50), MC);
534:            BinanceMinimumExecutionPolicy.Evaluation minimumCheck =
535:                    binanceMinimumExecutionPolicy.evaluate(symbol, soldQty, price);
536:            if (!minimumCheck.executable()) {
537:                // Same as Production: a below-minimum Binance order is not simulated. Reset
538:                // to rejection monitoring and let all existing management continue unchanged.
539:                current = current.withNearTp(new NearTpFailureProtectionPolicy.State(
540:                        NearTpState.NEAR_TP_REJECTION_DETECTED, current.nearTpBestPrice(),
541:                        0, current.nearTpLastOneMinuteSignalId(), false));
542:                persistNearTpState(current);
543:                log.info("FIX-11T Replay NEAR_TP_HARVEST_SKIPPED: runId={}, positionId={}, symbol={}, reason={}, requestedQty={}, requestedNotional={}, binanceMinimum={}; existing position management continues",
544:                        runId, current.positionId(), symbol, minimumCheck.code(), soldQty,
545:                        minimumCheck.requestedNotional(), minimumCheck.minimumNotional());
546:                return new LivePriceEvaluation(ExitDecision.hold(), current);
547:            }
548:            BigDecimal proceeds = soldQty.multiply(price, MC);
549:            BigDecimal soldCost = current.entryPrice().multiply(soldQty, MC);
550:            BigDecimal partialPnl = proceeds.subtract(soldCost, MC);
551:            BigDecimal partialPct = soldCost.signum() == 0 ? BigDecimal.ZERO : partialPnl
552:                    .divide(soldCost, 8, RoundingMode.HALF_UP).multiply(BigDecimal.valueOf(100));
553:            current = current.withPartialHarvest(soldQty, soldCost, partialPnl);
554:            persistNearTpPartialHarvest(runId, symbol, event, oneMinute, current, soldQty, proceeds, partialPnl, partialPct, nearTp.explanation());
555:            log.info("FIX-11T Replay NEAR_TP_PARTIAL_HARVESTED: runId={}, positionId={}, symbol={}, soldQty={}, remainingQty={}, price={}, partialPnl={}",
556:                    runId, current.positionId(), symbol, soldQty, current.quantity(), price, partialPnl);
557:            return new LivePriceEvaluation(ExitDecision.hold(), current, proceeds, partialPnl);
558:        }
559:        return new LivePriceEvaluation(ExitDecision.hold(), current);
560:    }
650:        }
651:        // Production has two SELL authorities: the live HTF PositionExitPolicy and the
652:        // 1m signal TradeExecutionValidationService. Replay executes both shared production
653:        // policies in the same order instead of carrying a test-only SELL rule.
654:        PositionExitPolicy.Evaluation normalExit = exitPolicy.evaluateNormalExit(
655:                oneMinute != null ? oneMinute : s, five, one);
656:        if (normalExit.exit()) {
657:            return new ExitDecision(true, normalExit.code(), normalExit.explanation());
658:        }
659:        if ("1m".equals(s.getInterval()) && bearish(s.getDecision())) {
660:            TradeExecutionValidationService.ValidationResult validatedSell = executionValidationService.validateSell(s);
661:            if (validatedSell.allowed()) {
662:                return new ExitDecision(true, validatedSell.code(), validatedSell.explanation());
663:            }
664:        }
665:        return ExitDecision.hold();
666:    }
667:
668:    private FallbackNearTpEvaluation evaluateNearTpAtSignalFallback(long runId, ShadowPosition current,
669:                                                                    TradeSignal signal, TradeSignal oneMinute,
670:                                                                    TradeSignal fiveMinute) {
671:        NearTpFailureProtectionPolicy.State state = new NearTpFailureProtectionPolicy.State(
672:                current.nearTpState(), current.nearTpBestPrice(), current.nearTpBearishStreak(),
673:                current.nearTpLastOneMinuteSignalId(), current.nearTpHarvestUsed());
674:        NearTpFailureProtectionPolicy.Evaluation evaluation = nearTpFailureProtectionPolicy.evaluate(
675:                state, current.entryPrice(), current.takeProfit(), signal.getLatestPrice(), signal.getGeneratedAt(),
676:                oneMinute, fiveMinute);
677:        ShadowPosition updated = current.withNearTp(evaluation.state());
678:        if (!updated.sameNearTp(current)) persistNearTpState(updated);
679:        if (evaluation.transition()) {
680:            log.info("FIX-11T Replay fallback Near-TP: runId={}, positionId={}, symbol={}, code={}, state={}, price={}, givebackPct={}, bearish1mStreak={}, detail={}",
681:                    runId, updated.positionId(), signal.getSymbol(), evaluation.code(), updated.nearTpState(),
682:                    signal.getLatestPrice(), evaluation.givebackPercent(), updated.nearTpBearishStreak(), evaluation.explanation());
683:        }
684:        if (!evaluation.harvestEligible()) return new FallbackNearTpEvaluation(updated, BigDecimal.ZERO, BigDecimal.ZERO);
685:
686:        BigDecimal soldQty = updated.quantity().multiply(BigDecimal.valueOf(0.50), MC);
687:        BinanceMinimumExecutionPolicy.Evaluation minimumCheck =
688:                binanceMinimumExecutionPolicy.evaluate(signal.getSymbol(), soldQty, signal.getLatestPrice());
689:        if (!minimumCheck.executable()) {
690:            updated = updated.withNearTp(new NearTpFailureProtectionPolicy.State(
691:                    NearTpState.NEAR_TP_REJECTION_DETECTED, updated.nearTpBestPrice(),
692:                    0, updated.nearTpLastOneMinuteSignalId(), false));
693:            persistNearTpState(updated);
694:            log.info("FIX-11T Replay fallback NEAR_TP_HARVEST_SKIPPED: runId={}, positionId={}, symbol={}, reason={}, requestedQty={}, requestedNotional={}, binanceMinimum={}; existing position management continues",
695:                    runId, updated.positionId(), signal.getSymbol(), minimumCheck.code(), soldQty,
696:                    minimumCheck.requestedNotional(), minimumCheck.minimumNotional());
697:            return new FallbackNearTpEvaluation(updated, BigDecimal.ZERO, BigDecimal.ZERO);
698:        }
699:        BigDecimal proceeds = soldQty.multiply(signal.getLatestPrice(), MC);
700:        BigDecimal soldCost = updated.entryPrice().multiply(soldQty, MC);
701:        BigDecimal pnl = proceeds.subtract(soldCost, MC);
702:        BigDecimal pnlPct = soldCost.signum() == 0 ? BigDecimal.ZERO : pnl.divide(soldCost, 8, RoundingMode.HALF_UP)
703:                .multiply(BigDecimal.valueOf(100));
704:        updated = updated.withPartialHarvest(soldQty, soldCost, pnl);
705:        persistNearTpPartialHarvestAtSignal(runId, signal, updated, soldQty, proceeds, pnl, pnlPct, evaluation.explanation());
706:        log.info("FIX-11T Replay fallback NEAR_TP_PARTIAL_HARVESTED: runId={}, positionId={}, symbol={}, soldQty={}, remainingQty={}, price={}, partialPnl={}",
707:                runId, updated.positionId(), signal.getSymbol(), soldQty, updated.quantity(), signal.getLatestPrice(), pnl);
708:        return new FallbackNearTpEvaluation(updated, proceeds, pnl);
709:    }

1018:    private BigDecimal totalPositionPnlPercent(ShadowPosition position, BigDecimal remainingLegPnl) {
1019:        BigDecimal totalPnl = position.partialRealizedPnl().add(remainingLegPnl, MC);
1020:        BigDecimal totalInvestedCost = position.cost().add(position.partialHarvestCostBasis(), MC);
1021:        return totalInvestedCost.signum() == 0 ? BigDecimal.ZERO
1022:                : totalPnl.divide(totalInvestedCost, 8, RoundingMode.HALF_UP).multiply(BigDecimal.valueOf(100));
1023:    }

85:    public boolean requestStop(long runId) {
86:        if (!activeRunIds.contains(runId)) return false;
87:        stopRequestedRunIds.add(runId);
88:        return true;
89:    }
90:
91:    public boolean isStopRequested(long runId) {
92:        return stopRequestedRunIds.contains(runId);
93:    }
94:
95:    private void checkStopRequested(long runId) {
96:        if (stopRequestedRunIds.contains(runId)) throw new ReplayCancellationException(runId);
97:    }

99:    @Async
100:    public void runAsync(long runId, ReplayDataSource dataSource) {
101:        // FIX-087: close the race at worker entry as well. Even if two callers schedule the same
102:        // run almost simultaneously, only one may execute the deterministic replay pipeline.
103:        if (!activeRunIds.add(runId)) {
104:            log.warn("FIX-087: replay run {} already has an active worker; duplicate execution skipped", runId);
105:            return;
106:        }
107:        // FIX-11J: Replay-only observational timers. The worker pipeline below is intentionally
108:        // left in its existing order; these locals only capture elapsed monotonic time and are
109:        // persisted once in finally so diagnostics cannot steer or fail Replay business logic.
110:        final long replayTotalStartedNs = System.nanoTime();
111:        Long loadHistoricalDataNs = null;
112:        Long verifyEventResolutionNs = null;
113:        Long buildReplayDatasetNs = null;
114:        Long generateFreshSignalsNs = null;
115:        Long shadowExecutionNs = null;
116:        Long parityComparisonNs = null;
117:        try (var inputAudit = sharedMarketEnabled ? com.crypto.shared.CandleInputAudit.open(jdbcTemplate,auditTransactionManager,"REPLAY:"+runId) : null) {
118:            long stageStartedNs = System.nanoTime();
119:            Map<String, Object> run = jdbcTemplate.queryForMap(
120:                    "SELECT symbol, start_time, end_time FROM analysis_test_run WHERE id = ?", runId);
121:            String symbol = String.valueOf(run.get("symbol"));
122:            Instant start = toInstant(run.get("start_time"));
123:            Instant end = toInstant(run.get("end_time"));
124:
125:            updateRun(runId, "RUNNING", 2, "Loading historical candles and signals", null);
126:            checkStopRequested(runId);
127:
128:            Instant contextStart = start.minus(REPLAY_CONTEXT_WARMUP);
129:            List<Candle> oneMinuteCandles = candles(symbol, "1m", contextStart, end);
130:            List<Candle> fiveMinuteCandles = candles(symbol, "5m", contextStart, end);
131:            List<Candle> oneHourCandles = candles(symbol, "1h", contextStart, end);
132:            List<Candle> requestedOneMinuteCandles = candlesInExecutionWindow(oneMinuteCandles, "1m", start, end);
133:            List<Candle> requestedFiveMinuteCandles = candlesInExecutionWindow(fiveMinuteCandles, "5m", start, end);
134:            List<Candle> requestedOneHourCandles = candlesInExecutionWindow(oneHourCandles, "1h", start, end);
135:
136:            String btcSymbol = btcContextProperties.referenceSymbol();
137:            boolean replayBtcContext = btcContextProperties.enabled() && !btcSymbol.equalsIgnoreCase(symbol);
138:            List<Candle> btcOneMinuteCandles = replayBtcContext ? candles(btcSymbol, "1m", contextStart, end) : List.of();
139:            List<Candle> btcFiveMinuteCandles = replayBtcContext ? candles(btcSymbol, "5m", contextStart, end) : List.of();
140:            List<Candle> btcOneHourCandles = replayBtcContext ? candles(btcSymbol, "1h", contextStart, end) : List.of();
141:
142:            List<TradeSignal> sourceSignals = signalRepository
143:                    .findBySymbolAndGeneratedAtBetweenOrderByGeneratedAtAsc(symbol, start, end);
144:
145:            jdbcTemplate.update("UPDATE analysis_test_run SET source_signal_count=? WHERE id=?",
146:                    sourceSignals.size(), runId);
147:            loadHistoricalDataNs = System.nanoTime() - stageStartedNs;
148:
149:            stageStartedNs = System.nanoTime();
150:            updateRun(runId, "RUNNING", 10, "Replaying event-candle resolution (no production writes)", null);
151:            checkStopRequested(runId);
152:            int replay1m = verifyEventResolution(runId, symbol, "1m", requestedOneMinuteCandles, 10, 28);
153:            int replay5m = verifyEventResolution(runId, symbol, "5m", requestedFiveMinuteCandles, 28, 38);
154:            int replay1h = verifyEventResolution(runId, symbol, "1h", requestedOneHourCandles, 38, 42);
155:            verifyEventResolutionNs = System.nanoTime() - stageStartedNs;
156:
157:            updateRun(runId, "RUNNING", 43, "Generating fresh signals from historical candles", null);
158:            checkStopRequested(runId);
159:            // FIX-11H: OLD=DATABASE and NEW=DATASET are explicit replay-only modes. Production
160:            // has no dependency on this selector. verifyEventResolution() intentionally remains unchanged.
161:            ReplayDataset replayDataset;
162:            if (dataSource == ReplayDataSource.DATASET) {
163:                stageStartedNs = System.nanoTime();
164:                replayDataset = loadDataset(symbol, btcSymbol, contextStart, end);
165:                buildReplayDatasetNs = System.nanoTime() - stageStartedNs;
166:            } else {
167:                // DATABASE mode has no ReplayDataset build stage. Persist NULL so the UI can
168:                // distinguish "not applicable" from a fabricated zero-duration measurement.
169:                replayDataset = null;
170:            }
171:            log.info("FIX-11H replay data source: run={}, mode={}, symbol={}, start={}, end={}",
172:                    runId, dataSource, symbol, start, end);
173:            stageStartedNs = System.nanoTime();
174:            FreshReplayStats fresh = generateFreshSignals(
175:                    runId, symbol, start, end,
176:                    oneMinuteCandles, fiveMinuteCandles, oneHourCandles,
177:                    btcSymbol, btcOneMinuteCandles, btcFiveMinuteCandles, btcOneHourCandles,
178:                    dataSource, replayDataset);
179:            jdbcTemplate.update("""
180:                    UPDATE analysis_test_run
181:                    SET generated_signal_count=?, generated_buy_count=?, generated_watch_count=?,
182:                        generated_sell_count=?, generated_strong_sell_count=?
183:                    WHERE id=?
184:                    """, fresh.total(), fresh.buys(), fresh.watches(), fresh.sells(), fresh.strongSells(), runId);
185:            generateFreshSignalsNs = System.nanoTime() - stageStartedNs;
186:
187:            stageStartedNs = System.nanoTime();
188:            updateRun(runId, "RUNNING", 74, "Running full shadow-production execution flow", null);
189:            checkStopRequested(runId);
190:            // FIX-052: Replay position protection consumes the same canonical live 1m
191:            // price observations that Production consumed. Historical windows before
192:            // V64 was deployed naturally have no events and are explicitly degraded to
193:            // signal/candle-close protection inside ShadowProductionReplayService.
194:            // FIX-153: validate the entire price window consumed, including context warm-up.
195:            marketPriceEventService.assertReplayEvidence(symbol,contextStart,end);
196:            var priceOutcomeCounts=marketPriceEventService.replayOutcomeCounts(symbol,contextStart,end);
197:            List<MarketPriceEventService.PriceEvent> productionPriceEvents =
198:                    marketPriceEventService.find(symbol, contextStart, end);
199:            ShadowProductionReplayService.ReplayStats shadow = shadowReplayService.replay(
200:                    runId, symbol, start, end, fresh.generatedSignals(), productionPriceEvents,
201:                    () -> isStopRequested(runId));
202:            // FIX-109: persist the parity contract with the run. A SIGNAL_PRICE_FALLBACK
203:            // result is useful historical evidence, but must never be presented as tick-exact
204:            // Production reproduction. Production parity is the default logic mode.
205:            jdbcTemplate.update("UPDATE analysis_test_run SET replay_price_mode=?, replay_logic_mode=? WHERE id=?",
206:                    shadow.priceReplayMode(), shadow.logicMode(), runId);
207:            checkStopRequested(runId);
208:            shadowExecutionNs = System.nanoTime() - stageStartedNs;
209:
210:            stageStartedNs = System.nanoTime();
211:            updateRun(runId, "RUNNING", 82, "Comparing historical decision authority", null);
212:            checkStopRequested(runId);
213:            int authorityCorrections = 0;
214:            int replaySignals = 0;
215:            int oldHardReversals = 0;
216:            int correctedHardReversals = 0;
217:
218:            for (TradeSignal signal : sourceSignals) {
219:                checkStopRequested(runId);
220:                SignalDecision finalDecision = signal.getDecision();
221:                SignalDecision original = signal.getOriginalDecision();
222:                SignalDecision effective = finalDecision != null ? finalDecision : original;
223:                boolean corrected = finalDecision != null && original != null
224:                        && isBearish(original) && !isBearish(finalDecision);
225:                if (corrected) authorityCorrections++;
520:            Candle candle = candles.get(i);
521:            List<Candle> latestAtEvent = candleRepository.findClosedCandlesAtOrBefore(
522:                    symbol, interval, candle.getOpenTime(), PageRequest.of(0, 1));
523:            if (!latestAtEvent.isEmpty() && candle.getOpenTime().equals(latestAtEvent.get(0).getOpenTime())) {
524:                resolved++;
525:            }
526:            if (i % 25 == 0 && size > 0) {
527:                int p = progressStart + (int) Math.round((progressEnd - progressStart) * (i / (double) size));
528:                updateRun(runId, "RUNNING", p,
529:                        "Replaying " + interval + " candle " + (i + 1) + "/" + size, null);
530:            }
531:        }
532:        return resolved;
533:    }
534:
535:    private FreshReplayStats generateFreshSignals(
536:            long runId,
537:            String symbol,
538:            Instant executionStart,
539:            Instant executionEnd,
540:            List<Candle> oneMinuteCandles,
541:            List<Candle> fiveMinuteCandles,
542:            List<Candle> oneHourCandles,
543:            String btcSymbol,
544:            List<Candle> btcOneMinuteCandles,
545:            List<Candle> btcFiveMinuteCandles,
546:            List<Candle> btcOneHourCandles,
547:            ReplayDataSource dataSource,
548:            ReplayDataset replayDataset
549:    ) {
550:        List<ReplayCandle> timeline = new java.util.ArrayList<>();
551:        oneMinuteCandles.forEach(c -> timeline.add(new ReplayCandle(symbol, "1m", c, true)));
552:        fiveMinuteCandles.forEach(c -> timeline.add(new ReplayCandle(symbol, "5m", c, true)));
553:        oneHourCandles.forEach(c -> timeline.add(new ReplayCandle(symbol, "1h", c, true)));
554:        btcOneMinuteCandles.forEach(c -> timeline.add(new ReplayCandle(btcSymbol, "1m", c, false)));
555:        btcFiveMinuteCandles.forEach(c -> timeline.add(new ReplayCandle(btcSymbol, "5m", c, false)));
556:        btcOneHourCandles.forEach(c -> timeline.add(new ReplayCandle(btcSymbol, "1h", c, false)));
557:        timeline.sort(java.util.Comparator
558:                .comparing((ReplayCandle rc) -> rc.candle().getOpenTime().plusSeconds(intervalSeconds(rc.interval())))
559:                .thenComparingInt(rc -> intervalOrder(rc.interval()))
560:                .thenComparingInt(rc -> rc.primary() ? 1 : 0));
561:
562:        int oneMinuteSignals = 0, fiveMinuteSignals = 0, oneHourSignals = 0;
563:        int oneMinuteBuys = 0, fiveMinuteBuys = 0, oneHourBuys = 0;
564:        int buys = 0, watches = 0, sells = 0, strongSells = 0, errors = 0;
565:        java.util.List<TradeSignal> generatedSignals = new java.util.ArrayList<>();
566:
567:        // FIX-11L: Replay-only deep profiling for the Generate fresh signals stage.
568:        // These monotonic timers observe existing calls only; they do not alter inputs,
569:        // call ordering, persistence, thresholds, or any Production trading behavior.
570:        ReplayFreshSignalProfiler profiler = new ReplayFreshSignalProfiler(runId, dataSource);
571:        // FIX-11M: Enable aggregate timing only for this Replay worker thread.
572:        // The shared AnalysisService decision path is unchanged; this is observation-only.
573:        analysisService.beginReplayAnalysisProfiling(runId);
574:        try (ExecutionReplayScope.Scope ignored = replayScope.open(runId, List.of(), o -> {})) {
575:        // FIX-043 Replay parity: fresh Replay has always generated technical snapshots/signals
576:        // chronologically for EVERY closed candle in the timeline. Production now restores the
577:        // same no-gap contract with asynchronous close-event processing plus chronological recovery.
578:        // Do not "optimize" Replay by sampling every Nth candle or by copying the production recovery
579:        // scheduler cadence; that would recreate the exact 1m->~5m blind-gap incident FIX-043 fixes.
580:        for (int index = 0; index < timeline.size(); index++) {
581:            checkStopRequested(runId);
582:            ReplayCandle replay = timeline.get(index);
583:            Candle candle = replay.candle();
584:            Instant evaluationTime = candle.getOpenTime().plusSeconds(intervalSeconds(replay.interval()));
585:            boolean inRequestedWindow = !evaluationTime.isBefore(executionStart) && !evaluationTime.isAfter(executionEnd);
586:            try {
587:                long operationStartedNs = System.nanoTime();
588:                java.util.Optional<IndicatorSnapshot> snapshot;
589:                try {
590:                    snapshot = dataSource == ReplayDataSource.DATASET
591:                            ? technicalIndicatorService.calculateSnapshotForRegression(
592:                                    replay.symbol(), replay.interval(), candle.getOpenTime(), replayDataset)
593:                            : technicalIndicatorService.calculateSnapshotForRegression(
594:                                    replay.symbol(), replay.interval(), candle.getOpenTime());
595:                } finally {
596:                    profiler.recordSnapshot(System.nanoTime() - operationStartedNs);
597:                }
598:                if (snapshot.isEmpty()) {
599:                    if (inRequestedWindow) {
600:                        errors++;
601:                        saveGenerationError(runId, replay.symbol(), replay.interval(), candle,
602:                                "Not enough historical candles to calculate the technical snapshot");
603:                    }
604:                    continue;
605:                }
606:
607:                // Do not generate a signal whose candle would only have closed after the
608:                // requested test end. Warm-up rows before executionStart are intentionally
609:                // generated for as-of context, but are not counted or displayed as test output.
610:                if (evaluationTime.isAfter(executionEnd)) continue;
611:
612:                replayScope.reference(evaluationTime);
613:                operationStartedNs = System.nanoTime();
614:                TradeSignal generated;
615:                try {
616:                    generated = analysisService.analyzeForRegression(snapshot.get(), evaluationTime);
617:                } finally {
618:                    profiler.recordAnalysis(System.nanoTime() - operationStartedNs);
619:                }
620:                replayScope.appendSignal(generated);
621:                SignalDecision decision = generated.getDecision();
622:                if (replay.primary()) generatedSignals.add(generated);
623:
624:                if (replay.primary() && inRequestedWindow) {
625:                    if ("1m".equals(replay.interval())) { oneMinuteSignals++; if (isBuy(decision)) oneMinuteBuys++; }
626:                    else if ("5m".equals(replay.interval())) { fiveMinuteSignals++; if (isBuy(decision)) fiveMinuteBuys++; }
627:                    else if ("1h".equals(replay.interval())) { oneHourSignals++; if (isBuy(decision)) oneHourBuys++; }
628:
629:                    if (decision == SignalDecision.BUY || decision == SignalDecision.STRONG_BUY) buys++;
630:                    else if (decision == SignalDecision.WATCH) watches++;
631:                    else if (decision == SignalDecision.SELL) sells++;
632:                    else if (decision == SignalDecision.STRONG_SELL) strongSells++;
633:
634:                    operationStartedNs = System.nanoTime();
635:                    try {
636:                        jdbcTemplate.update("""
637:                            INSERT INTO analysis_test_signal
638:                                (test_run_id, source_signal_id, replay_generated, symbol, interval_code, candle_open_time,
639:                                 generated_at, latest_price, original_decision, final_decision,
640:                                 execution_effective_decision, total_score, confidence_score, raw_confidence_score,
641:                                 effective_confidence_score, primary_blocking_stage, detected_regime, candidate_regime,
642:                                 confirmed_regime, regime_candidate_count, entry_authority, entry_authority_max_position_percent,
643:                                 trend_score, volume_score, momentum_score, decision_authority_corrected, generation_error)
644:                            VALUES (?, NULL, 1, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, 0, NULL)
645:                            """,
646:                            runId, replay.symbol(), replay.interval(), Timestamp.from(candle.getOpenTime()),
647:                            Timestamp.from(evaluationTime), generated.getLatestPrice(),
648:                            generated.getOriginalDecision() == null ? null : generated.getOriginalDecision().name(),
649:                            decision == null ? null : decision.name(),
650:                            decision == null ? null : decision.name(),
651:                            generated.getTotalScore(), generated.getConfidenceScore(), generated.getRawConfidenceScore(),
652:                            generated.getEffectiveConfidenceScore(), generated.getPrimaryBlockingStage(),
653:                            generated.getDetectedRegime() == null ? null : generated.getDetectedRegime().name(),
654:                            generated.getCandidateRegime() == null ? null : generated.getCandidateRegime().name(),
655:                            generated.getConfirmedRegime() == null ? null : generated.getConfirmedRegime().name(),
656:                            generated.getRegimeCandidateCount(), generated.getEntryAuthority(),
657:                            generated.getEntryAuthorityMaxPositionPercent(), generated.getTrendScore(),
658:                            generated.getVolumeScore(), generated.getMomentumScore());
659:                    } finally {
660:                        profiler.recordAnalysisTestSignalPersistence(System.nanoTime() - operationStartedNs);
661:                    }
662:
663:                    // FIX-069: Persist the exact production TradeSignal shape as replay output.
664:                    // analysis_test_signal remains for backward-compatible diagnostics, while
665:                    // trade_signal_test is the canonical parity table used for field-by-field comparison.
666:                    // FIX-112D: Replay lineage uses exact market-candle identity, never
667:                    // generated_at or nearest-time matching. A legitimate Replay-only
668:                    // signal remains NULL. This metadata lookup cannot alter Production.
669:                    operationStartedNs = System.nanoTime();
670:                    Long sourceSignalId;
671:                    try {
672:                        sourceSignalId = dataSource == ReplayDataSource.DATASET
673:                                ? replayDataset.sourceSignalId(
674:                                        generated.getSymbol(), generated.getInterval(), generated.getCandleOpenTime())
675:                                : signalRepository.findBySymbolAndIntervalAndCandleOpenTime(
676:                                        generated.getSymbol(), generated.getInterval(), generated.getCandleOpenTime())
677:                                        .map(TradeSignal::getId).orElse(null);
678:                    } finally {
679:                        profiler.recordLineageLookup(System.nanoTime() - operationStartedNs);
680:                    }
681:                    operationStartedNs = System.nanoTime();
682:                    try {
683:                        persistTradeSignalTest(runId, generated, sourceSignalId, null);
684:                    } finally {
685:                        profiler.recordTradeSignalTestPersistence(System.nanoTime() - operationStartedNs);
686:                    }
687:                }
688:            } catch (ReplayCancellationException stop) {
689:                throw stop;
690:            } catch (Exception exception) {
691:                if (replay.primary() && inRequestedWindow) {
692:                    errors++;
693:                    saveGenerationError(runId, replay.symbol(), replay.interval(), candle, errorDetail(exception, 900));
694:                }
695:                log.warn("Regression fresh-signal generation failed: run={}, symbol={}, interval={}, candle={}",
696:                        runId, replay.symbol(), replay.interval(), candle.getOpenTime(), exception);
697:            }
698:
699:            if (index % 10 == 0 && !timeline.isEmpty()) {
700:                int progress = 43 + (int) Math.round(30d * index / timeline.size());
701:                long progressStartedNs = System.nanoTime();
702:                try {
703:                    updateRun(runId, "RUNNING", Math.min(73, progress),
704:                            "Generating fresh signal " + (index + 1) + "/" + timeline.size(), null);
705:                } finally {
706:                    profiler.recordProgressUpdate(System.nanoTime() - progressStartedNs);
707:                }
708:            }
709:        }
710:
150:        if (!("PENDING".equals(status) || "RUNNING".equals(status))) {
151:            return Map.of("id", runId, "status", status, "activeWorker", worker.isActive(runId),
152:                    "message", "Test is already stopped.");
153:        }
154:        boolean requested = worker.requestStop(runId);
155:        if (!requested) {
156:            // No worker in this JVM means it is already orphaned/stopped. Mark it ERROR now so
157:            // Delete Data is not blocked forever by a stale RUNNING database status.
158:            jdbcTemplate.update("""
159:                    UPDATE analysis_test_run
160:                    SET status='ERROR', current_step='Stopped by user',
161:                        error_message='Replay worker was not active in this application instance; run marked stopped.',
162:                        completed_at=CURRENT_TIMESTAMP(6), heartbeat_at=CURRENT_TIMESTAMP(6)
163:                    WHERE id=? AND status IN ('PENDING','RUNNING')
164:                    """, runId);
165:            return Map.of("id", runId, "status", "ERROR", "activeWorker", false,
166:                    "message", "Test had no active worker and is now stopped. You can Delete Data.");
167:        }
168:        jdbcTemplate.update("UPDATE analysis_test_run SET current_step='Stop requested ƒ?" waiting for replay worker to exit', heartbeat_at=CURRENT_TIMESTAMP(6) WHERE id=?", runId);
169:        return Map.of("id", runId, "status", "STOPPING", "activeWorker", true,
170:                "message", "Stop requested. Delete Data will unlock after the replay worker has fully stopped.");
171:    }

177:    /** FIX-11H explicit replay-only OLD/NEW selector. Production is not configurable through this mode. */
178:    public synchronized long start(RegressionTestRunRequest request, ReplayDataSource dataSource) {
179:        if (request == null || request.symbol() == null || request.symbol().isBlank()
180:                || request.startTime() == null || request.endTime() == null
181:                || !request.endTime().isAfter(request.startTime())) {
182:            throw new IllegalArgumentException("Symbol, start time and end time are required, and end must be after start.");
183:        }
184:
185:        Integer activeRuns = jdbcTemplate.queryForObject("""
186:                SELECT COUNT(*)
187:                FROM analysis_test_run
188:                WHERE status IN ('PENDING', 'RUNNING')
189:                """, Integer.class);
190:        if (activeRuns != null && activeRuns > 0) {
191:            Map<String, Object> active = jdbcTemplate.queryForMap("""
192:                    SELECT id, test_name, symbol, status, progress_percent, started_at, created_at
193:                    FROM analysis_test_run
194:                    WHERE status IN ('PENDING', 'RUNNING')
195:                    ORDER BY id DESC
196:                    LIMIT 1
197:                    """);
198:            throw new ResponseStatusException(HttpStatus.CONFLICT,
199:                    "Regression test #" + active.get("id") + " is already " + active.get("status")
200:                            + " for " + active.get("symbol") + ". Wait for it to finish before starting another run.");
201:        }
202:
203:        String symbol = request.symbol().trim().toUpperCase(Locale.ROOT);
204:        ReplayDataSource effectiveSource = dataSource == null ? ReplayDataSource.DATABASE : dataSource;
205:        String baseTestName = request.testName() == null || request.testName().isBlank()
206:                ? symbol + " regression" : request.testName().trim();
207:        String testName = (baseTestName + " [" + effectiveSource.name() + "]").substring(
208:                0, Math.min(180, baseTestName.length() + effectiveSource.name().length() + 3));
209:
210:        KeyHolder keyHolder = new GeneratedKeyHolder();
211:        jdbcTemplate.update(connection -> {
212:            PreparedStatement statement = connection.prepareStatement("""
213:                    INSERT INTO analysis_test_run
214:                        (test_name, symbol, start_time, end_time, status, progress_percent, current_step)
215:                    VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, 'PENDING', 0, 'Queued from Administration')
216:                    """, Statement.RETURN_GENERATED_KEYS);
217:            statement.setString(1, testName);
218:            statement.setString(2, symbol);
219:            statement.setTimestamp(3, Timestamp.from(request.startTime()));
220:            statement.setTimestamp(4, Timestamp.from(request.endTime()));
221:            return statement;
222:        }, keyHolder);
223:
224:        Number key = keyHolder.getKey();
225:        if (key == null) {
226:            throw new IllegalStateException("Could not create regression test run.");
227:        }
228:        long id = key.longValue();
229:        // FIX-122: persist the business-rule revision separately from data-source/experimental modes.
230:        jdbcTemplate.update("INSERT INTO fix122_replay_revision(test_run_id,enabled) VALUES (?,?)",

33:    @PostMapping("/investigation-cases/{id}/run")
34:    @ResponseStatus(HttpStatus.ACCEPTED)

80:    /** Exact event lineage prevents a caller from completing a different receipt.
81:     * A lost resolver race rolls back the entire effects transaction. Slot 1 is retained. */
82:    public void finish(long id,String symbol,Instant observed,BigDecimal price,String state,String reason){
83:        price=normalizePrice(price);
84:        if(!org.springframework.transaction.support.TransactionSynchronizationManager.isActualTransactionActive())
85:            throw new IllegalStateException("FIX-153 price outcome requires effects transaction");

88:        if(n!=1)throw new IllegalStateException("FIX-153 price outcome ownership/lineage changed: event="+id);

103:    public void resolvePending(){

107:            var bounded=new JdbcTemplate(java.util.Objects.requireNonNull(jdbcTemplate.getDataSource()));bounded.setQueryTimeout(2);

156:        if("OFF".equals(mode))return;

158:        if(cutovers!=null && cutovers>0) {

160:                Long.class,symbol,Timestamp.from(from),Timestamp.from(to));

162:        }

24:@Service
25:@RequiredArgsConstructor
26:public class DerivativesPositioningService {
27:    private static final BigDecimal ONE_HUNDRED = new BigDecimal("100");
28:
29:    private final BinanceFuturesMarketDataClient client;
30:    private final DerivativesPositioningProperties properties;
31:    private final CandleRepository candleRepository;
32:
33:    /**
34:     * Historical replay must never call the live Binance futures endpoints.
35:     * Derivatives history is not persisted by this application, so the only
36:     * truthful replay result is UNAVAILABLE for that historical timestamp.
37:     */
38:    public DerivativesPositioningResult evaluateHistorical(String symbol, String interval, SignalDecision decision,
39:                                                            boolean entryAllowed, Instant evaluatedAt) {
40:        Instant time = evaluatedAt == null ? Instant.now() : evaluatedAt;
41:        if (!properties.enabled()) {
42:            return result(DerivativesPositioningStatus.NOT_APPLICABLE, decision, entryAllowed,
43:                    null, null, null, null, null, null, 0, period(interval), 0,
44:                    "Derivatives positioning is disabled.", time);
45:        }
46:        return result(DerivativesPositioningStatus.UNAVAILABLE, decision, entryAllowed,
47:                null, null, null, null, null, null, 0, period(interval), 0,
48:                "Historical derivatives positioning was not persisted; live Binance data is intentionally not used during replay.",
49:                time);
50:    }
51:
52:    public DerivativesPositioningResult evaluate(String symbol, String interval, SignalDecision decision,
53:                                                  boolean entryAllowed, Instant evaluatedAt) {
54:        Instant now = evaluatedAt == null ? Instant.now() : evaluatedAt;
55:        if (!properties.enabled()) return result(DerivativesPositioningStatus.NOT_APPLICABLE, decision, entryAllowed,
56:                null, null, null, null, null, null, 0, period(interval), 0,
57:                "Derivatives positioning is disabled.", now);
58:        try {
59:            List<BinanceFundingRate> funding = client.fundingHistory(symbol, properties.fundingHistoryLimit());
60:            List<BinanceOpenInterestPoint> oi = client.openInterestHistory(symbol, period(interval), properties.openInterestHistoryLimit());
61:            if (funding.isEmpty() || oi.size() < 2) {
62:                return result(DerivativesPositioningStatus.LEARNING, decision, entryAllowed,
63:                        latestFunding(funding), percentile(funding), latestOi(oi), latestOiValue(oi), null,
64:                        priceChange(symbol, interval), funding.size(), period(interval), -5,
65:                        "Funding or open-interest history is still insufficient; no veto was applied.", now);
66:            }
67:            BigDecimal fundingRate = latestFunding(funding);
68:            BigDecimal fundingPercentile = percentile(funding);
69:            BigDecimal oiChange = percentChange(oi.get(oi.size() - 2).sumOpenInterest(), oi.get(oi.size() - 1).sumOpenInterest());
70:            BigDecimal priceChange = priceChange(symbol, interval);
71:            DerivativesPositioningStatus status = classify(fundingRate, oiChange, priceChange);
72:            SignalDecision finalDecision = decision;
73:            boolean allowed = entryAllowed;
74:            int confidence = confidence(status);
75:            if (isBullish(decision) && status == DerivativesPositioningStatus.LONGS_CROWDED
76:                    && properties.vetoExtremeCrowding()
77:                    && oiChange != null && oiChange.abs().compareTo(properties.strongOpenInterestChangeThreshold()) >= 0) {
78:                finalDecision = SignalDecision.WATCH;
79:                allowed = false;
80:            }
81:            if (isBearish(decision) && status == DerivativesPositioningStatus.SHORTS_CROWDED
82:                    && properties.vetoExtremeCrowding()

112:     * with no persisted evidence remain explicitly UNAVAILABLE.
113:     */
114:    public OrderBookLiquidityResult evaluateHistorical(
115:            String symbol,
116:            String interval,
117:            SignalDecision currentDecision,
118:            boolean entryAllowed,
119:            BigDecimal currentPrice,
120:            BigDecimal stopLoss,
121:            BigDecimal takeProfit,
122:            Instant evaluatedAt
123:    ) {

125:        Instant snapshotTime = evaluatedAt == null ? Instant.now() : evaluatedAt;
126:        if (!properties.enabled()) {
127:            return result(LiquidityContextStatus.DISABLED, currentDecision, currentDecision,
128:                    entryAllowed, null, null, null, null, null, null, null, null,
129:                    false, false, 0, "Order-book liquidity analysis is disabled.", snapshotTime,
130:                    policy, 0L);
131:        }
132:
133:        // FIX-112C: historical Replay reads only persisted Production observations
134:        // whose observed_at is inside the exact as-of window. No live Binance book
135:        // and no future observation can enter a historical decision.
136:        Instant windowStart = snapshotTime.minusSeconds(policy.windowSeconds());
137:        List<NormalizedObservation> observations = orderBookSnapshotService
138:                .find(symbol, windowStart, snapshotTime).stream()
139:                .map(row -> new NormalizedObservation(row.observedAt(),
140:                        // FIX-112C parity preservation: an invalid persisted snapshot must remain
141:                        // in the observation sequence. Pre-FIX-112 Production counted every snapshot
142:                        // in the active window and, critically, returned UNAVAILABLE when the latest
143:                        // snapshot itself had no valid midpoint. Dropping invalid rows here would
144:                        // silently change that established Production behavior during Replay.
145:                        row.bestBid() == null || row.bestAsk() == null
146:                                || row.bestBid().add(row.bestAsk()).signum() <= 0
147:                                ? null
148:                                : new SnapshotMetrics(
149:                                        row.bidDepth(), row.askDepth(), row.depthImbalance(),
150:                                        row.spreadBps() == null ? null : row.spreadBps().divide(ONE_HUNDRED, MC),
151:                                        row.bidWallPrice(), row.bidWallQuantity(),
152:                                        row.askWallPrice(), row.askWallQuantity())))
153:                .toList();
154:        if (observations.isEmpty()) {
155:            return result(LiquidityContextStatus.UNAVAILABLE, currentDecision, currentDecision,
156:                    entryAllowed, null, null, null, null, null, null, null, null,
157:                    false, false, 0,
158:                    "No persisted Production order-book observation exists for this historical replay window; live data was not substituted.",
159:                    snapshotTime, policy, 0L);
160:        }
161:        return evaluateNormalized(symbol, interval, currentDecision, entryAllowed,
162:                currentPrice, stopLoss, takeProfit, snapshotTime, policy, observations);
163:    }
164:
165:    public OrderBookLiquidityResult evaluate(
166:            String symbol,
167:            String interval,
168:            SignalDecision currentDecision,
169:            boolean entryAllowed,
170:            BigDecimal currentPrice,
171:            BigDecimal stopLoss,
172:            BigDecimal takeProfit,
173:            Instant evaluatedAt
174:    ) {
175:        IntervalPolicy policy = properties.policyFor(interval == null ? "5m" : interval);
176:        Instant snapshotTime = evaluatedAt == null ? Instant.now() : evaluatedAt;
177:        if (!properties.enabled()) {
178:            return result(LiquidityContextStatus.DISABLED, currentDecision, currentDecision,
179:                    entryAllowed, null, null, null, null, null, null, null, null,
180:                    false, false, 0, "Order-book liquidity analysis is disabled.", snapshotTime,
181:                    policy, 0L);
182:        }
183:
184:        String normalizedSymbol = normalize(symbol);
185:        Deque<OrderBookSnapshot> deque = historyBySymbol.get(normalizedSymbol);
186:        if (deque == null || deque.isEmpty()) {
187:            collectSafely(normalizedSymbol);
188:            deque = historyBySymbol.get(normalizedSymbol);
189:        }
190:        if (deque == null || deque.isEmpty()) {
191:            // FIX-091 / Fix 2: in LIVE analysis, zero observations are not proof that liquidity is safe.
192:            // Hold a fresh entry until the configured sample minimum is reached. Historical replay uses
193:            // evaluateHistorical(...) and deliberately keeps its separate UNAVAILABLE limitation.
194:            return result(LiquidityContextStatus.INSUFFICIENT_DATA_HOLD, currentDecision, currentDecision,
195:                    false, null, null, null, null, null, null, null, null,
196:                    false, false, 0, "Live order-book sampling is not ready (0/" + policy.minimumObservations()
197:                            + "). Fresh entry is held until enough observations are collected.", snapshotTime,
198:                    policy, 0L);
199:        }
200:
201:        List<OrderBookSnapshot> snapshots;
202:        synchronized (deque) {
203:            Instant windowStart = snapshotTime.minusSeconds(policy.windowSeconds());
204:            snapshots = deque.stream()
205:                    .filter(snapshot -> !snapshot.capturedAt().isAfter(snapshotTime))
206:                    .filter(snapshot -> !snapshot.capturedAt().isBefore(windowStart))
207:                    .toList();
208:        }
209:        if (snapshots.isEmpty()) {
210:            // FIX-091 / Fix 2: an enabled LIVE order-book service with no observation in the active
250:        // Before the shared-evaluator refactor, live Production inspected ONLY the latest
251:        // snapshot's midpoint first. If that latest reading was invalid, the entire Order Book
252:        // evaluation returned UNAVAILABLE even when older snapshots in the window were valid.
253:        // Keep that exact gate explicitly in the shared path so Replay conforms to Production;
254:        // tolerating/filtering an invalid latest snapshot is a separate trading-behavior change
255:        // and must never ride along inside this parity/infrastructure fix.
256:        SnapshotMetrics latestMetrics = observations.get(observationCount - 1).metrics();
257:        if (latestMetrics == null) {
258:            return result(LiquidityContextStatus.UNAVAILABLE, currentDecision, currentDecision,
259:                    entryAllowed, null, null, null, null, null, null, null, null,
260:                    false, false, observationCount, "Order-book best bid/ask is unavailable.",
261:                    snapshotTime, policy, observedSeconds);
262:        }
263:
264:        if (observationCount < policy.minimumObservations()) {
265:            // FIX-112C exact parity: once historical observations exist, partial sampling
266:            // must reproduce Production's INSUFFICIENT_DATA_HOLD rather than becoming a
267:            // Replay-only UNAVAILABLE pass. Only a completely absent historical window is
268:            // reported UNAVAILABLE by evaluateHistorical().
269:            return result(LiquidityContextStatus.INSUFFICIENT_DATA_HOLD,
270:                    currentDecision, currentDecision, false,
271:                    latestMetrics.imbalance(), latestMetrics.bidDepth(), latestMetrics.askDepth(),
272:                    latestMetrics.spreadPercent(), latestMetrics.bidWallPrice(), latestMetrics.bidWallSize(),
273:                    latestMetrics.askWallPrice(), latestMetrics.askWallSize(), false, false,
274:                    observationCount,
275:                    "Collecting " + interval + " liquidity observations (" + observationCount + "/"
276:                            + policy.minimumObservations() + ") inside a " + policy.windowSeconds()
277:                            + " second window. Fresh entry is held until the minimum sample is ready.",
278:                    snapshotTime, policy, observedSeconds);
279:        }
280:
281:        PersistentWall bidWall = persistentWallNormalized(observations, true, policy);
282:        PersistentWall askWall = persistentWallNormalized(observations, false, policy);
283:        boolean targetBlocked = isBuy(currentDecision)
284:                && currentPrice != null && takeProfit != null && askWall != null
285:                && askWall.price().compareTo(currentPrice) > 0
===== src/main/java/com/crypto/wallet/service/BinanceMinimumExecutionPolicy.java =====
1:package com.crypto.wallet.service;
2:
3:import com.crypto.client.binance.BinanceMarketDataClient;
4:import lombok.RequiredArgsConstructor;
5:import lombok.extern.slf4j.Slf4j;
6:import org.springframework.stereotype.Component;
7:
8:import java.math.BigDecimal;
9:import java.math.MathContext;
10:import java.util.Locale;
11:
12:/**
13: * FIX-11T minimum-executable-order guard shared by Production Paper and Replay.
14: *
15: * The project intentionally models Paper/Replay as real Binance trading with only the
16: * final connector missing.  Therefore a Near-TP partial harvest that Binance would reject
17: * for being below the symbol's minimum notional is not simulated.  No alternative exit
18: * size is invented: the position simply continues through its existing management logic.
19: *
20: * This class deliberately checks only the agreed minimum executable notional boundary.
21: * It does not add a new Near-TP-specific step-size/precision model.
22: */
23:@Component
24:@RequiredArgsConstructor
25:@Slf4j
26:public class BinanceMinimumExecutionPolicy {
27:    private static final MathContext MC = MathContext.DECIMAL64;
28:
29:    private final BinanceMarketDataClient binanceMarketDataClient;
30:    public Evaluation evaluate(String symbol, BigDecimal quantity, BigDecimal price) {
31:        if (symbol == null || symbol.isBlank() || quantity == null || quantity.signum() <= 0
32:                || price == null || price.signum() <= 0) {
33:            return new Evaluation(false, "INVALID_ORDER_INPUT", BigDecimal.ZERO, null);
34:        }
35:        String pair = symbol.trim().toUpperCase(Locale.ROOT);
36:        BigDecimal notional = quantity.multiply(price, MC);
37:        BigDecimal minimum;
38:        try {
39:            minimum = binanceMarketDataClient.getMinimumExecutableNotional(pair);
40:        } catch (RuntimeException ex) {
41:            // Fail safe. Missing exchange constraints must never be converted into permission
42:            // to create an order that real Binance might reject.
43:            log.warn("FIX-11T Binance minimum executable amount unavailable: symbol={}, requestedNotional={}, error={}",
44:                    pair, notional, ex.getMessage());
45:            return new Evaluation(false, "BINANCE_MINIMUM_UNAVAILABLE", notional, null);
46:        }
47:        if (notional.compareTo(minimum) < 0) {
48:            return new Evaluation(false, "BELOW_BINANCE_MINIMUM", notional, minimum);
49:        }
50:        return new Evaluation(true, "EXECUTABLE", notional, minimum);
51:    }
52:
53:    public record Evaluation(boolean executable, String code, BigDecimal requestedNotional, BigDecimal minimumNotional) {
54:    }
55:}

80:                                           String executionExplanation, String entryStage, int entryQualityScore) {
81:        if (signal == null || signal.getId() == null) return false;
82:        int requestedPositionPercent = Math.max(1, Math.min(100, positionPercent));
83:
84:        String pair = normalizePair(signal.getSymbol());

87:                .findFirstBySymbolAndStatusOrderByOpenedAtDesc(pair, "OPEN")
88:                .orElse(null);

90:        // FIX-125: position precedes mutation; statistics precede cash on every exit.
580:                .orElse(null);
581:        if(walletCoordination!=null)walletCoordination.mutation();
582:        // FIX-125: position precedes mutation; statistics precede cash on every exit.
583:        dailyStatisticsRepository.findForUpdateByTradeDate(LocalDate.now(ZoneOffset.UTC));
584:
585:        if (position == null || position.getId() == null || position.getQuantity() == null
586:                || position.getQuantity().signum() <= 0) {
587:            return PartialHarvestResult.notExecuted("NO_OPEN_POSITION");
588:        }
589:
590:        String key = "POSITION:" + position.getId() + ":NEAR_TP_PARTIAL_HARVEST";
591:        if (executionExists(key)) {
592:            return PartialHarvestResult.notExecuted("ALREADY_EXECUTED");
593:        }
594:        if (position.isNearTpHarvestUsed()) {
595:            return PartialHarvestResult.notExecuted("HARVEST_ALREADY_USED");
596:        }
597:
598:        String assetSymbol = pair.substring(0, pair.length() - 4);
599:        if(walletCoordination!=null)getOrCreate("USDT");
600:        WalletAsset coin = getOrCreate(assetSymbol);
601:        BigDecimal requested = position.getQuantity().multiply(BigDecimal.valueOf(0.50), MC);
602:        BigDecimal quantity = requested.min(position.getQuantity()).min(coin.getQuantity());
603:        if (quantity.signum() <= 0) return PartialHarvestResult.notExecuted("NO_SELLABLE_QUANTITY");

605:        BigDecimal price = positive(executionPrice);
606:        // FIX-11T: Paper must respect the same Binance minimum executable order boundary
607:        // as a future real connector. If this 50% reduction is below the symbol minimum,
608:        // do nothing; never promote it to a larger/full exit.

613:                    position.getId(), pair, minimumCheck.code(), quantity, minimumCheck.requestedNotional(), minimumCheck.minimumNotional());

618:        BigDecimal costBasis = position.getAverageEntryPriceUsdt().multiply(quantity, MC);
619:        BigDecimal realized = gross.subtract(costBasis, MC);
620:        BigDecimal realizedPercent = costBasis.signum() == 0 ? ZERO : realized

625:        assetRepository.save(coin);

628:        }

632:        BigDecimal remainingCost = position.getTotalCostUsdt().subtract(costBasis, MC);
633:        if (remainingQuantity.signum() <= 0) {
634:            throw new IllegalStateException("Near-TP partial harvest must not close the entire managed position");
635:        }

16:/**
17: * Thread-bound data/persistence override used only by Administration historical replay.
18: *
19: * The execution algorithms remain the production services. While this scope is active,
20: * their signal lookups are resolved exclusively from replay-generated signals as-of the
21: * current replay timestamp, and opportunity persistence is redirected to the supplied
22: * shadow sink. Production repositories are never read/written by the scoped execution path.
23: */
24:@Component
25:public class ExecutionReplayScope {
26:
27:    /** FIX-109: Production parity is the default replay contract. Experimental mode is
28:     * explicit so replay-only candidate rules can never contaminate a parity result. */
29:    public enum ReplayLogicMode { PRODUCTION_PARITY, EXPERIMENTAL }
30:
31:    private final ThreadLocal<State> state = new ThreadLocal<>();
32:
33:    public Scope open(long runId, List<TradeSignal> replaySignals, Consumer<ExecutionOpportunity> opportunitySink) {
34:        return open(runId, replaySignals, opportunitySink, ReplayLogicMode.PRODUCTION_PARITY);
35:    }
36:
37:    public Scope open(long runId, List<TradeSignal> replaySignals, Consumer<ExecutionOpportunity> opportunitySink,
38:                      ReplayLogicMode logicMode) {
39:        if (state.get() != null) throw new IllegalStateException("Execution replay scope is already active on this thread");
40:        List<TradeSignal> immutable = replaySignals == null ? List.of() : replaySignals.stream()
41:                .filter(s -> s != null && s.getGeneratedAt() != null)
42:                .sorted(Comparator.comparing(TradeSignal::getGeneratedAt))
43:                .toList();
44:        state.set(new State(runId, immutable, opportunitySink, logicMode == null ? ReplayLogicMode.PRODUCTION_PARITY : logicMode));
45:        return new Scope();
46:    }
47:
48:    /** FIX-122: run-local executed boundaries, independent of Production ledger/history. */
49:    public void recordStop(String symbol, long positionId, Instant at, String reason) {
50:        if (!"STOP_LOSS".equals(reason)) return;
51:        State s=required();
52:        StopLossEvidencePolicy.Boundary previous=s.stops.get(symbol);
53:        if (previous==null || !at.isBefore(previous.executedAt()))
54:            s.stops.put(symbol,new StopLossEvidencePolicy.Boundary(positionId,at));
55:    }
56:    public StopLossEvidencePolicy.Boundary stopBoundary(String symbol, Instant asOf) {
57:        StopLossEvidencePolicy.Boundary b=required().stops.get(symbol);
58:        return b!=null && !b.executedAt().isAfter(asOf) ? b : null;
59:    }
60:    public Instant referenceInstant() { return required().reference; }
61:    public boolean fix122Enabled() { return required().fix122Enabled; }
62:    public void fix122Enabled(boolean value) { required().fix122Enabled=value; }
63:
64:    public boolean active() { return state.get() != null; }
65:    public long runId() { return required().runId; }
66:    public ReplayLogicMode logicMode() { return required().logicMode; }
67:    public boolean experimental() { return active() && required().logicMode == ReplayLogicMode.EXPERIMENTAL; }
68:    public boolean productionParity() { return active() && required().logicMode == ReplayLogicMode.PRODUCTION_PARITY; }
69:

94:    /**

96:     * uses this incrementally so the production confluence/BTC services can resolve only
97:     * context that had genuinely been generated by the current replay timestamp.

100:        if (signal == null || signal.getGeneratedAt() == null) return;
