106:    public List<TradeSignal> recent(String symbol, String interval, Instant reference, int limit) {
107:        State s = required();
108:        Instant ref = reference != null ? reference : s.reference;
109:        if (ref == null) return List.of();
110:        return s.signals.stream()
111:                .filter(x -> symbol.equals(x.getSymbol()) && interval.equals(x.getInterval()))
112:                .filter(x -> !x.getGeneratedAt().isAfter(ref))
113:                .sorted(Comparator.comparing(TradeSignal::getGeneratedAt).reversed())
114:                .limit(limit)
115:                .toList();
116:    }
117:
118:    public Optional<TradeSignal> latestAtOrBefore(String symbol, String interval, Instant reference) {
119:        return recent(symbol, interval, reference, 1).stream().findFirst();
120:    }
121:
122:    /**
123:     * FIX-141: Replay-side equivalent of
124:     * TradeSignalRepository#findTopBySymbolAndIntervalAndCandleOpenTimeLessThanEqualAndGeneratedAtLessThanEqualOrderByCandleOpenTimeDesc.
125:     * Selects by candle open time (close-time ordering), not generatedAt, so Replay cannot diverge
126:     * from Production on which BTC snapshot it picks. candleOpenTimeBound is the caller's look-ahead
127:     * bound (reference minus the interval duration); generatedAt must also be at or before reference.
128:     */
129:    public Optional<TradeSignal> latestClosedAtOrBefore(String symbol, String interval, Instant reference, Instant candleOpenTimeBound) {
130:        State s = required();
131:        if (reference == null || candleOpenTimeBound == null) return Optional.empty();
132:        return s.signals.stream()
133:                .filter(x -> symbol.equals(x.getSymbol()) && interval.equals(x.getInterval()))
134:                .filter(x -> x.getGeneratedAt() != null && !x.getGeneratedAt().isAfter(reference))
135:                .filter(x -> x.getCandleOpenTime() != null && !x.getCandleOpenTime().isAfter(candleOpenTimeBound))
136:                .max(Comparator.comparing(TradeSignal::getCandleOpenTime));
137:    }
138:
139:    public Optional<TradeSignal> previousBefore(String symbol, String interval, Instant reference) {
140:        State s = required();
141:        return s.signals.stream()
142:                .filter(x -> symbol.equals(x.getSymbol()) && interval.equals(x.getInterval()))
143:                .filter(x -> x.getGeneratedAt().isBefore(reference))
144:                .max(Comparator.comparing(TradeSignal::getGeneratedAt));
145:    }
146:
147:    public Optional<ExecutionOpportunity> currentOpportunity(String symbol, List<String> statuses) {
148:        ExecutionOpportunity o = required().opportunity;
149:        if (o == null || !symbol.equals(o.getSymbol())) return Optional.empty();
150:        if (statuses != null && !statuses.isEmpty() && !statuses.contains(o.getStatus())) return Optional.empty();
151:        return Optional.of(o);
152:    }
153:
154:    public ExecutionOpportunity saveOpportunity(ExecutionOpportunity opportunity) {
155:        State s = required();
156:        s.opportunity = opportunity;
157:        if (s.opportunitySink != null) s.opportunitySink.accept(opportunity);
158:        return opportunity;
159:    }
160:
161:    public final class Scope implements AutoCloseable {
162:        private boolean closed;
163:        @Override public void close() {
164:            if (!closed) {
165:                state.remove();
166:                closed = true;
167:            }
168:        }
169:    }
170:
171:    private State required() {
172:        State s = state.get();
173:        if (s == null) throw new IllegalStateException("No Administration execution replay scope is active");
174:        return s;
175:    }
176:
177:    public record ReplayPrice(String symbol, Instant observedAt, java.math.BigDecimal price) {}
178:
179:    private static final class State {
180:        final long runId;
181:        final List<TradeSignal> signals;
182:        final Consumer<ExecutionOpportunity> opportunitySink;
183:        final ReplayLogicMode logicMode;
184:        boolean fix122Enabled = true;
185:        final java.util.Map<String,StopLossEvidencePolicy.Boundary> stops = new java.util.HashMap<>();
186:        Instant reference;
187:        ExecutionOpportunity opportunity;
188:        ReplayPrice latestPrice;
189:        final Set<Long> consumedSignalIds = new HashSet<>();
190:        State(long runId, List<TradeSignal> signals, Consumer<ExecutionOpportunity> opportunitySink, ReplayLogicMode logicMode) {
191:            this.runId = runId;
192:            this.signals = new ArrayList<>(signals);
193:            this.opportunitySink = opportunitySink;
194:            this.logicMode = logicMode;
195:        }
196:    }
197:}

> 8:## 1. Business objective
  9:
  10:Make manual Replay reproduce the current paper-trading Production business flow
  11:as closely as durable historical evidence permits, while remaining completely
  28:
> 29:The result is not a claim that historical Java scheduling, MySQL lock timing,
  30:network delay, external Binance availability, or the historical Production
  31:wallet balance can be reproduced. It is a verified **business-logic parity**
> 32:claim only when all required market/decision evidence exists. Otherwise Replay
  33:remains available as a labelled simulation and may not claim parity.
  34:
  35:## 2. Confirmed root cause and evidence
  74:- Live-exchange orders, fills, fees, slippage, or order-book execution modelling.
> 75:- Replaying database locks, thread scheduling, JVM pauses, WebSocket transport,
  76:  external REST failures, or transaction retries as historical facts.

> 80:## 4. Contractual modes
  81:
  82:| Mode | Purpose | Missing evidence behaviour | Claim allowed |
  83:|---|---|---|---|
> 84:| `BUSINESS_LOGIC_PARITY` | Reproduce Production rules using an explicitly selected virtual Replay account and 
complete market/decision evidence. | Refuse the run before execution. | Business-logic parity. |
  85:| `SIMULATION` | Manual what-if/research run. | Continue but persist all approximation reasons. | Simulation 
only. |
  86:| `EXPERIMENTAL_RESEARCH` | Explicit, separately approved research such as FIX-155. | Per experiment contract. | 
Never Production parity. |

1:package com.crypto.wallet.service;
2:
3:import com.crypto.client.binance.BinanceMarketDataClient;
4:import lombok.RequiredArgsConstructor;
5:import lombok.extern.slf4j.Slf4j;
6:import org.springframework.stereotype.Component;
7:
8:import java.math.BigDecimal;
9:import java.math.MathContext;
10:import java.util.Locale;
11:
12:/**
13: * FIX-11T minimum-executable-order guard shared by Production Paper and Replay.
14: *
15: * The project intentionally models Paper/Replay as real Binance trading with only the
16: * final connector missing.  Therefore a Near-TP partial harvest that Binance would reject
17: * for being below the symbol's minimum notional is not simulated.  No alternative exit
18: * size is invented: the position simply continues through its existing management logic.
19: *
20: * This class deliberately checks only the agreed minimum executable notional boundary.
21: * It does not add a new Near-TP-specific step-size/precision model.
22: */
23:@Component
24:@RequiredArgsConstructor
25:@Slf4j
26:public class BinanceMinimumExecutionPolicy {
27:    private static final MathContext MC = MathContext.DECIMAL64;
28:
29:    private final BinanceMarketDataClient binanceMarketDataClient;
30:    public Evaluation evaluate(String symbol, BigDecimal quantity, BigDecimal price) {
31:        if (symbol == null || symbol.isBlank() || quantity == null || quantity.signum() <= 0
32:                || price == null || price.signum() <= 0) {
33:            return new Evaluation(false, "INVALID_ORDER_INPUT", BigDecimal.ZERO, null);
34:        }
35:        String pair = symbol.trim().toUpperCase(Locale.ROOT);
36:        BigDecimal notional = quantity.multiply(price, MC);
37:        BigDecimal minimum;
38:        try {
39:            minimum = binanceMarketDataClient.getMinimumExecutableNotional(pair);
40:        } catch (RuntimeException ex) {
41:            // Fail safe. Missing exchange constraints must never be converted into permission
42:            // to create an order that real Binance might reject.
43:            log.warn("FIX-11T Binance minimum executable amount unavailable: symbol={}, requestedNotional={}, error={}",
44:                    pair, notional, ex.getMessage());
45:            return new Evaluation(false, "BINANCE_MINIMUM_UNAVAILABLE", notional, null);
46:        }
47:        if (notional.compareTo(minimum) < 0) {
48:            return new Evaluation(false, "BELOW_BINANCE_MINIMUM", notional, minimum);
49:        }
50:        return new Evaluation(true, "EXECUTABLE", notional, minimum);
51:    }
52:
53:    public record Evaluation(boolean executable, String code, BigDecimal requestedNotional, BigDecimal minimumNotional) {
54:    }
55:}

## EVIDENCE

- **PROJECT EVIDENCE:** Replay is currently single-symbol by request and execution: `RegressionTestRunRequest` has one `symbol`; the worker reads one symbol and loads its three candle lanes. [RegressionTestRunRequest.java](C:/workspace/crypto-ai-trader-mysql/src/main/java/com/crypto/regression/dto/RegressionTestRunRequest.java:5), [RegressionTestWorker.java](C:/workspace/crypto-ai-trader-mysql/src/main/java/com/crypto/regression/service/RegressionTestWorker.java:119)

- **PROJECT EVIDENCE:** Historical derivatives are deliberately always `UNAVAILABLE` when enabled, so default parity mode would correctly refuse all affected historical runs unless new durable evidence is introduced. [DerivativesPositioningService.java](C:/workspace/crypto-ai-trader-mysql/src/main/java/com/crypto/service/DerivativesPositioningService.java:33)

- **PROJECT EVIDENCE:** Current event retrieval orders SQL rows by `observed_at, id`, but converts them to a `PriceEvent` that omits the ID. Signal ordering is timestamp plus interval only. [MarketPriceEventService.java](C:/workspace/crypto-ai-trader-mysql/src/main/java/com/crypto/market/service/MarketPriceEventService.java:118), [ShadowProductionReplayService.java](C:/workspace/crypto-ai-trader-mysql/src/main/java/com/crypto/regression/service/ShadowProductionReplayService.java:65)

- **PROJECT EVIDENCE:** FIX-11K/FIX-11Q are active Replay references, not merely dormant tables: the replay service invokes both observers and the admin controller exposes FIX-11K observations. [ShadowProductionReplayService.java](C:/workspace/crypto-ai-trader-mysql/src/main/java/com/crypto/regression/service/ShadowProductionReplayService.java:100), [RegressionTestController.java](C:/workspace/crypto-ai-trader-mysql/src/main/java/com/crypto/regression/controller/RegressionTestController.java:120)

## OBJECTIONS

- **PROJECT EVIDENCE:** RPR-0.4 does not meet the required implementation-contract granularity. RPR-01 through RPR-12 are work areas, not a complete modify/create/delete/config/migration/test registry; neither `ShadowProductionReplayService`, `RegressionTestWorker`, `RegressionTestService`, controller/UI, wallet services, observers, repositories, nor exact migrations are assigned authorized method-level changes. This permits unregistered refactoring and prevents later reconciliation. [agent-docs/FINAL_CHANGE_SPECIFICATION.md](C:/workspace/crypto-ai-trader-mysql/agent-docs/FINAL_CHANGE_SPECIFICATION.md:218)

- **AGENT INFERENCE:** “Complete price coverage” is not operationally defined. The draft requires every needed observation and gaps, but defines neither expected event cadence, maximum permitted gap, WebSocket-disconnect evidence, nor how a missing observation is detected. An accepted-event count cannot prove that no unrecorded price would have crossed TP/SL/profit-lock. A parity certificate could therefore be falsely positive. [agent-docs/FINAL_CHANGE_SPECIFICATION.md](C:/workspace/crypto-ai-trader-mysql/agent-docs/FINAL_CHANGE_SPECIFICATION.md:107)

- **AGENT INFERENCE:** The pre-execution rejection contract is incomplete. Eligibility requires coverage from the first Replay open through terminal close, but those boundaries emerge only after signal generation and shadow execution. The specification must define a pure, non-persisting preflight/dry-run phase, its inputs, and refusal behavior; otherwise it conflicts with its own “before generated signals or shadow wallet state are written” requirement. [agent-docs/FINAL_CHANGE_SPECIFICATION.md](C:/workspace/crypto-ai-trader-mysql/agent-docs/FINAL_CHANGE_SPECIFICATION.md:93)

- **PROJECT EVIDENCE:** The certificate lacks a defined oracle. “Field comparisons” does not specify the compared production records, identity keys, fields, precision/tolerance, missing-record handling, configuration identity, or how a virtual account can be compared with a deliberately non-reproduced historical wallet. This is material because the current Replay has a synthetic account and one-symbol lifecycle. [agent-docs/FINAL_CHANGE_SPECIFICATION.md](C:/workspace/crypto-ai-trader-mysql/agent-docs/FINAL_CHANGE_SPECIFICATION.md:232), [ShadowProductionReplayService.java](C:/workspace/crypto-ai-trader-mysql/src/main/java/com/crypto/regression/service/ShadowProductionReplayService.java:75)

- **PROJECT EVIDENCE:** Deterministic ordering remains under-specified. The draft says to use event IDs and a tie order, but does not define ordering among simultaneous 1m/5m/1h signals, signal/work commit sequence, or the schema/API change needed to retain the required identifiers. Current Replay’s `PriceEvent` has no ID. [agent-docs/FINAL_CHANGE_SPECIFICATION.md](C:/workspace/crypto-ai-trader-mysql/agent-docs/FINAL_CHANGE_SPECIFICATION.md:187), [MarketPriceEventService.java](C:/workspace/crypto-ai-trader-mysql/src/main/java/com/crypto/market/service/MarketPriceEventService.java:173)

- **AGENT INFERENCE:** The removal scope is contradictory: section 6 calls FIX-11K/11Q removals “candidates,” while RPR-09 authorizes their execution without deciding retain-versus-remove per referenced API, UI, service, migration, and archive path. Each must have a fixed disposition before approval. [agent-docs/FINAL_CHANGE_SPECIFICATION.md](C:/workspace/crypto-ai-trader-mysql/agent-docs/FINAL_CHANGE_SPECIFICATION.md:203)

## UNKNOWNS

- **PROJECT EVIDENCE:** No production system, database, or runtime logs were accessed. I therefore cannot establish whether historical price, order-book, derivatives, configuration, or version evidence actually exists for any proposed parity window.

- **AGENT INFERENCE:** No parity claim should be approved until the next revision defines the evidence completeness algorithm, certificate oracle, deterministic total ordering, and full authorized file/method/migration registry.
